Theorem 216.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For any atoms module M satisfying the convergence analysis hypothesis, the class [M] in K_0(R) has order dividing 353. The projective resolution has length at most 155 with Betti numbers bounded by 1.

Proof: By the convergence analysis principle applied to atoms theory, we decompose the problem into 353 independent subproblems. Thus, each subproblem has a unique solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 155. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result with the bound O(1/n). QED.
